(() => {
  try {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const storedTheme = localStorage.getItem("bk-theme");
    const themeMode =
      storedTheme === "light" || storedTheme === "dark" || storedTheme === "system"
        ? storedTheme
        : "dark";
    const storedDensity = localStorage.getItem("bk-density");
    const density = storedDensity === "comfortable" ? "comfortable" : "compact";
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    // v2 design system (2026-09-03): theme is a user choice on EVERY page.
    // Default is dark; "system" follows the OS; "light" is a real palette.
    const applyTheme = () => {
      const resolvedTheme = themeMode === "system" ? (media.matches ? "dark" : "light") : themeMode;
      root.setAttribute("data-theme", resolvedTheme);
      root.setAttribute("data-theme-mode", themeMode);
    };
    root.setAttribute("data-density", density);
    applyTheme();
  } catch {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", "dark");
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-density", "compact");
  }
})();

Nothing is stored; it never leaves your browser.</p></details></div></div><section class="mt-6"><div class="flex items-end justify-between gap-3 mb-2"><div><h2 class="text-[15px] font-semibold text-foreground leading-tight">How to read this</h2></div></div><p class="m-0 max-w-[90ch] text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">The heatmap is an estimate of where leveraged positions would be forced out, built from recorded taker flow and open interest across the major perpetual venues; levels the price has already swept are removed. It is not an exchange liquidation order book. The orange ticks and the 24h totals are real liquidations recorded from exchange streams. Each level is spread over a band that widens with its distance from price, because leverage is a distribution rather than five round numbers; the 30 day view averages the archive into four hour columns. The public view is the 24 hour window; members get the deeper windows, filters and playback.<!-- --> <a class="text-primary underline" href="/methodology">Methodology</a></p></section><section class="mt-6"><div class="flex items-end justify-between gap-3 mb-2"><div><h2 class="text-[15px] font-semibold text-foreground leading-tight">What a liquidation heatmap shows</h2></div></div><div class="grid gap-3 max-w-[90ch]"><p class="m-0 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">A liquidation heatmap plots, for every hour and every price, how much leveraged money would be forced to close there. Long positions liquidate below the current price, short positions above it. Bright bands are the clusters that price tends to be pulled toward, because filling them forces the market to trade through them.</p><p class="m-0 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">LiqMap builds the estimate from recorded taker flow and open interest across the major perpetual venues, removes the levels the price has already swept, and overlays the liquidations that actually fired from our own exchange streams. Every hour the map is archived, so the history you scroll through was recorded as it happened and cannot be backfilled.</p><p class="m-0 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">The public page shows the last 24 hours for every listed perpetual. Members get the 4 hour to 30 day windows, leverage and venue filters, hour-by-hour playback and the position check.</p></div></section><section class="mt-6"><div class="flex items-end justify-between gap-3 mb-2"><div><h2 class="text-[15px] font-semibold text-foreground leading-tight">FAQ</h2></div></div><div class="rounded-[var(--radius)] border border-border bg-surface divide-y divide-border-subtle"><details class="group px-4 py-2.5"><summary class="cursor-pointer list-none flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-[13.5px] font-medium text-foreground"><span>Is the heatmap real liquidation data?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="text-muted transition-transform group-open:rotate-45 text-[16px] leading-none">+</span></summary><div class="pt-2 pb-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-muted">The colour field is an estimate of where leveraged positions would liquidate, built from recorded flow and open interest. The orange ticks and the 24h totals are real liquidations recorded from exchange streams.</div></details><details class="group px-4 py-2.5"><summary class="cursor-pointer list-none flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-[13.5px] font-medium text-foreground"><span>Why do clusters move or disappear?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="text-muted transition-transform group-open:rotate-45 text-[16px] leading-none">+</span></summary><div class="pt-2 pb-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-muted">Levels the price has swept are removed, new positions add new levels, and the model recomputes every ten minutes. The hourly archive keeps every earlier frame, which is what the playback scrubs.</div></details><details class="group px-4 py-2.5"><summary class="cursor-pointer list-none flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-[13.5px] font-medium text-foreground"><span>What do members get?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="text-muted transition-transform group-open:rotate-45 text-[16px] leading-none">+</span></summary><div class="pt-2 pb-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-muted">The 4 hour to 30 day windows, leverage and venue filters, hour-by-hour playback and the position check. The 24 hour map stays public.</div></details></div></section><section class="mt-6"><div class="flex items-end justify-between gap-3 mb-2"><div><h2 class="text-[15px] font-semibold text-foreground
